  function statusPill() {
    if (status === "idle") return { txt: "Idle", cls: "statusPill" };
    if (status === "ready" || status === "success") {
      return { txt: status === "ready" ? "Klaar" : "Succes", cls: "statusPill ok" };
    }
    if (status === "waiting" || status === "busy") {
      return { txt: status === "waiting" ? "Wacht" : "Bezig", cls: "statusPill warn" };
    }
    return { txt: "Fout", cls: "statusPill err" };
  }

  function machinePill() {
    if (mrState === "ready") return { txt: "Ready", cls: "statusPill ok" };
    if (mrState === "printing") return { txt: "Printing", cls: "statusPill warn" };
    if (mrState === "error") return { txt: "Alarm", cls: "statusPill err" };
    if (mrState === "disconnected") return { txt: "Offline", cls: "statusPill err" };
    return { txt: mrState, cls: "statusPill" };
  }
>>>>>>> REPLACE
```

  <div class="topbar">
    <div>
      <h1>Vorm snijden</h1>
      <p class="submsg">Max {XY_MAX}×{XY_MAX} mm, XY-only G-code. Voorwaarde: X en Y gehomed.</p>

      </span>
    </div>

    <div class="errorBox">Klipper alarm:\n{mrMessage}</div>
  {/if}

  <div class="statusRow">
    <span class={statusPill().cls}>{statusPill().txt}</span>
    <span class="submsg">{statusMsg}</span>
  </div>

  {#if lastError}
    <div class="errorBox">{lastError}</div>
  {/if}

  {#if view === "picker"}
    <p class="submsg">Swipe horizontaal om een vorm te kiezen en tik om te configureren.</p>

    <div class="shapeCarousel">
      {#each shapes as s}
        <div
          class={"shapeCard " + (selected === s.id ? "selected" : "")}
          on:click={() => openConfig(s.id)}
        >

              <div class="cardSub">{s.subtitle}</div>
            </div>

            <div class="miniSvg" aria-hidden="true">
              {#if s.id === "circle"}
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                  <circle cx="60" cy="60" r="32" fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="4" />
                </svg>
              {:else if s.id === "rect"}
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                  <rect x="28" y="40" width="64" height="40" rx="6" fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="4" />
                </svg>
              {:else if s.id === "slot"}
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                  <rect x="28" y="46" width="64" height="28" rx="14" ry="14" fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="4" />
                </svg>
              {:else}
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                  <polygon
                    points="60,28 86,42 86,78 60,92 34,78 34,42"
                    fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="4" />
                </svg>
              {/if}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="cardSub">Tik om te openen</div>
        </div>
      {/each}
    </div>
  {:else}
    <div class="actions">
      <input type="file" accept=".gcode" on:change={handleFileChange} />
      <button class="primary" on:click={uploadFile}>Upload Shape File</button>
      <button class="ghost" on:click={backToPicker}>← Terug</button>
      <button class="primary" on:click={prepareCut}>Vorm snijden</button>

      {#if status === "waiting"}
        <button class="primary" on:click={startCutNow}>START (test)</button>
      {/if}
    </div>

    {#if preparedSummary}
      <p class="submsg">
        Voorbereid: <b>{preparedSummary}</b>
      </p>
    {/if}

    <div class="configGrid">
      <section class="panel">
        <h2 class="panelTitle">Preview</h2>

          {#if selected === "circle"}
            <svg class="bigSvg" viewBox="0 0 120 120">

              <line x1={60 - mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2} y1={60 + mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2 + 14}
                    x2={60 + mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2} y2={60 + mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2 + 14}
                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr)" marker-end="url(#arr)" />
              <text x="60" y={60 + mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2 + 28} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="800">
                Ø {circle.diameter.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>
            </svg>

              <defs>

                  <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="#b7c6ea"/>
                </marker>

              <rect x={box.x} y={box.y} width={box.w} height={box.h} rx="6"

                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr2)" marker-end="url(#arr2)" />
              <text x={box.cx} y={box.y + box.h + 24} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="800">
                L {rect.length.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>

              <line x1={box.x + box.w + 10} y1={box.y} x2={box.x + box.w + 10} y2={box.y + box.h}
                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr2)" marker-end="url(#arr2)" />
              <text x={box.x + box.w + 20} y={box.cy} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="start" font-weight="800">
                B {rect.width.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>
            </svg>

          {:else if selected === "slot"}
            {@const box = centerBoxSize(slot.length, slot.width)}
            <svg class="bigSvg" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
              <defs>
                <marker id="arr3" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="5" refY="5" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" orient="auto-start-reverse">
                  <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="#b7c6ea"/>
                </marker>
              </defs>

              <rect x={box.x} y={box.y} width={box.w} height={box.h} rx={box.h/2} ry={box.h/2}
                    fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="3" />

              <line x1={box.x} y1={box.y + box.h + 10} x2={box.x + box.w} y2={box.y + box.h + 10}
                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr3)" marker-end="url(#arr3)" />
              <text x={box.cx} y={box.y + box.h + 24} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="800">
                L {slot.length.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>

              <line x1={box.x + box.w + 10} y1={box.y} x2={box.x + box.w + 10} y2={box.y + box.h}
                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr3)" marker-end="url(#arr3)" />
              <text x={box.x + box.w + 20} y={box.cy} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="start" font-weight="800">
                B {slot.width.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>

              <text x={box.x + box.w - 2} y={box.y - 6} fill="#b7c6ea" font-size="9" text-anchor="end" font-weight="800">
                R = {(slot.width / 2).toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>
            </svg>

          {:else}
            <svg class="bigSvg" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
              <defs>
                <marker id="arr4" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="5" refY="5" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" orient="auto-start-reverse">
                  <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="#b7c6ea"/>
                </marker>
              </defs>

              <polygon points={hexPointsForPreview(hex.acrossFlats)} fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="3" />

              <text x="60" y="110" fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="800">
                S {hex.acrossFlats.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>
            </svg>
          {/if}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="panel">
        <h2 class="panelTitle">Maten</h2>

        <div class="fields">
          {#if selected === "circle"}
            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Diameter Ø (mm)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Diameter Ø (mm)",
                    value: circle.diameter,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      circle = { diameter: v };
                      normalizeCircle();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {circle.diameter.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

          {:else if selected === "rect"}
            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Lengte L (mm)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Lengte L (mm)",
                    value: rect.length,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      rect = { ...rect, length: v };
                      normalizeRect();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {rect.length.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Breedte B (mm)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Breedte B (mm)",
                    value: rect.width,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      rect = { ...rect, width: v };
                      normalizeRect();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {rect.width.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

          {:else if selected === "slot"}
            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Lengte L (mm)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Lengte L (mm)",
                    value: slot.length,
                    min: slot.width,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      slot = { ...slot, length: v };
                      normalizeSlot();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {slot.length.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Breedte B (mm) (R = B/2)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Breedte B (mm)",
                    value: slot.width,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      slot = { ...slot, width: v };
                      normalizeSlot();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {slot.width.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="hintRow">
              Radius automatisch: R = {(slot.width / 2).toFixed(1)} mm
            </div>

          {:else}
            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Steekmaat S (mm) (across flats)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Steekmaat S (mm)",
                    value: hex.acrossFlats,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      hex = { acrossFlats: v };
                      normalizeHex();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {hex.acrossFlats.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>
          {/if}

          <div class="hintRow">
            Begrensd op {XY_MAX} mm i.v.m. X/Y slag. XY moet gehomed zijn: <b>{isHomedXY() ? "JA" : "NEE"}</b>
          </div>

          {#if preparedGcode}
            <div class="hintRow">
              G-code voorbereid (preview eerste regels):
            </div>
            <div class="gcodePreview">
              {preparedGcode.split("\n").slice(0, 18).join("\n")}
            </div>
          {/if}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  {/if}
</div>

{#if padOpen}
  <div class="modalBack" on:click={padCancel}>
    <div class="modal" on:click|stopPropagation>
      <div class="modalHead">
        <div class="panelTitle">{padTitle}</div>
        <button class="ghost" on:click={padCancel}>Sluiten</button>
      </div>

      <div class="modalValue">{clamp(parseFloat(padValue || "0"), padMin, padMax).toFixed(1)} mm</div>
      <div class="submsg">Toegestaan: {padMin} – {padMax} mm</div>

      <div class="pad">
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("1")}>1</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("2")}>2</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("3")}>3</button>

        <button on:click={() => padAppend("4")}>4</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("5")}>5</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("6")}>6</button>

        <button on:click={() => padAppend("7")}>7</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("8")}>8</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("9")}>9</button>

        <button on:click={() => padAppend(".")}>.</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("0")}>0</button>
        <button on:click={padBackspace}>⌫</button>

        <button class="danger" on:click={padClear}>Clear</button>
        <button class="ghost" on:click={padCancel}>Cancel</button>
        <button class="wide primary" on:click={padOk}>Enter</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
{/if}

<style>
  :global(:root) {
    color-scheme: dark;
    --bg-body: #070b12;
    --bg-surface: #0c121d;
    --bg-surface-alt: #101828;
    --border-color: #1a2332;
    --border-color-strong: #243247;
    --accent: #6aa7ff;
    --accent-strong: #3c66c2;
    --text-strong: #e5e9f0;
    --text-medium: #bac7dd;
    --text-muted: #8895af;
    --radius-sm: 8px;
    --radius-md: 12px;
    --radius-lg: 16px;
    --space-1: 4px;
    --space-2: 6px;
    --space-3: 10px;
    --space-4: 14px;
    --space-5: 18px;
    --space-6: 24px;
    --space-7: 32px;
    --font-size-xs: 12px;
    --font-size-sm: 13px;
    --font-size-md: clamp(14px, 1.8vw, 15.5px);
    --font-size-lg: clamp(15px, 2vw, 17px);
    --font-size-xl: clamp(17px, 3vw, 20px);
  }

  :global(html, body) {
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    background: var(--bg-body);
    color: var(--text-strong);
    font-family: "Inter", system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Arial, sans-serif;
    font-size: var(--font-size-md);
    line-height: 1.45;
  }

  :global(*) {
    box-sizing: border-box;
  }

  .layout {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 56px 1fr;
    height: 100dvh;
    background: var(--bg-body);
  }

  .sidebar {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    border-right: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    padding: var(--space-4) var(--space-2);
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-3);
  }

  .logoDot {
    width: 38px;
    height: 38px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    background: radial-gradient(120% 120% at 20% 20%, #1a2436 0%, #0f1420 70%);
    border: 1px solid #1f2a3c;
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03);
    margin-bottom: var(--space-3);
  }

  .nav {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-2);
    width: 100%;
    align-items: center;
  }

  a.item {
    width: 42px;
    height: 42px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    text-decoration: none;
    color: var(--text-medium);
    border: 1px solid transparent;
    user-select: none;
    -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
    background: transparent;
    transition: background 0.14s ease, color 0.14s ease, border-color 0.14s ease;
  }

  a.item:hover {
    background: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.08);
    border-color: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.18);
    color: var(--text-strong);
  }

  a.item.active {
    background: rgba(60, 102, 194, 0.2);
    border-color: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.4);
    color: var(--text-strong);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.25) inset;
  }

  a.item:focus {
    outline: 2px solid var(--accent);
    outline-offset: 2px;
  }

  .icon {
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    display: block;
  }

  .stroke {
    stroke: currentColor;
    stroke-width: 1.8;
    fill: none;
    stroke-linecap: round;
    stroke-linejoin: round;
  }

  .fillSoft {
    fill: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
    stroke: currentColor;
    stroke-width: 1.8;
    stroke-linecap: round;
    stroke-linejoin: round;
  }

  .main {
    overflow: hidden;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-rows: 1fr;
  }

  .content {
    overflow: auto;
    padding: var(--space-5);
    width: min(100%, 760px);
    margin: 0 auto;
    -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
  }

  :global(.pageShell) {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-4);
    padding-bottom: var(--space-6);
  }

  :global(.surfaceCard) {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: var(--space-4);
    box-shadow: 0 4px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.18);
  }

  :global(.surfaceCard.tight) {
    padding: var(--space-3);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  }

  :global(.sectionTitle) {
    font-size: var(--font-size-xl);
    font-weight: 800;
    margin: 0 0 var(--space-2);
  }

  :global(.sectionLead) {
    color: var(--text-medium);
    font-size: var(--font-size-sm);
    margin: 0;
  }

  :global(.sectionHint) {
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-size: var(--font-size-sm);
    margin: 0;
  }

  :global(.statusPill),
  :global(.badgePill) {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-2);
    padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3);
    border-radius: 999px;
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    background: var(--bg-surface-alt);
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--text-medium);
  }

  :global(.statusPill.ok) {
    color: #a8f9c6;
    border-color: #215c39;
    background: rgba(33, 92, 57, 0.18);
  }

  :global(.statusPill.warn) {
    color: #ffe1a4;
    border-color: #6a5320;
    background: rgba(106, 83, 32, 0.18);
  }

  :global(.statusPill.err) {
    color: #ffb5b5;
    border-color: #7a1f1f;
    background: rgba(122, 31, 31, 0.18);
  }

  :global(.errorBox) {
    margin-top: var(--space-3);
    padding: var(--space-3);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    border: 1px solid #7a1f1f;
    background: #1b0d10;
    color: #ffb5b5;
    font-family: "JetBrains Mono", ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, "Liberation Mono", monospace;
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
    white-space: pre-wrap;
    max-height: 150px;
    overflow: auto;
  }

  :global(button) {
    background: var(--bg-surface-alt);
    color: var(--text-strong);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    padding: 10px 14px;
    font-weight: 700;
    font-size: var(--font-size-sm);
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background 0.14s ease, border-color 0.14s ease, transform 0.1s ease;
    min-height: 38px;
  }

  :global(button:hover) {
    background: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.12);
    border-color: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.3);
  }

  :global(button:active) {
    transform: translateY(1px);
  }

  :global(button:disabled) {
    opacity: 0.45;
    cursor: not-allowed;
  }

  :global(button.primary) {
    background: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.2);
    border-color: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.4);
  }

  :global(button.primary:hover) {
    background: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.28);
  }

  :global(button.secondary) {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    color: var(--text-medium);
  }

  :global(button.secondary:hover) {
    color: var(--text-strong);
  }

  :global(button.ghost) {
    background: transparent;
  }

  :global(button.danger) {
    background: rgba(122, 31, 31, 0.18);
    border-color: #7a1f1f;
    color: #ffb5b5;
  }

  :global(button.danger:hover) {
    background: rgba(122, 31, 31, 0.28);
  }

  :global(input[type="file"]) {
    max-width: 100%;
    font-size: var(--font-size-sm);
  }

<div class="wrap">
  <div class="card">
    <div class="title">Handbediening</div>

    <div class="hintline">
      Positie: X {toFixedNice(pos[0])} | Y {toFixedNice(pos[1])} | Z {toFixedNice(pos[2])}
      <br />
      Limits: X {positionMin[0]}..{positionMax[0]} • Y {positionMin[1]}..{positionMax[1]} • Z {positionMin[2]}..{positionMax[2]}
    </div>

    <div class="badges">
      <div class="badge">
        <span class="dot" class:ok={connected} class:bad={!connected}></span>
        {connected ? "Connected" : "Disconnected"}
      </div>

      <div class="badge">
        <span class="dot" class:ok={isAxisHomed("X")} class:bad={!isAxisHomed("X")}></span>
        X homed
      </div>
      <div class="badge">
        <span class="dot" class:ok={isAxisHomed("Y")} class:bad={!isAxisHomed("Y")}></span>
        Y homed
      </div>
      <div class="badge">
        <span class="dot" class:ok={isAxisHomed("Z")} class:bad={!isAxisHomed("Z")}></span>
        Z homed
      </div>
    </div>

    {#if lastError}
      <div class="errorBox">{lastError}</div>
    {/if}
  </div>

  <div class="card">
    <div class="title">Jog (mm)</div>

    <!-- X -->
    <div class="grid" style="margin-top: 10px;">
      <div class="axis">X</div>
      <div class="btns">
        <button disabled={!connected || !isAxisHomed("X")} on:click={() => jog("X", -1)}>X -</button>
        <button disabled={!connected || !isAxisHomed("X")} on:click={() => jog("X", 1)}>X +</button>
      </div>

      <div class="stepBox" role="button" tabindex="0" on:click={() => openKeypad("X")}>
        <span class="stepVal">{toFixedNice(stepX)}</span>
        <span class="unit">mm</span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Y -->
    <div class="grid" style="margin-top: 10px;">
      <div class="axis">Y</div>
      <div class="btns">
        <button disabled={!connected || !isAxisHomed("Y")} on:click={() => jog("Y", -1)}>Y -</button>
        <button disabled={!connected || !isAxisHomed("Y")} on:click={() => jog("Y", 1)}>Y +</button>
      </div>

      <div class="stepBox" role="button" tabindex="0" on:click={() => openKeypad("Y")}>
        <span class="stepVal">{toFixedNice(stepY)}</span>
        <span class="unit">mm</span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Z -->
    <div class="grid" style="margin-top: 10px;">
      <div class="axis">Z</div>
      <div class="btns">
        <button disabled={!connected || !isAxisHomed("Z")} on:click={() => jog("Z", -1)}>Z -</button>
        <button disabled={!connected || !isAxisHomed("Z")} on:click={() => jog("Z", 1)}>Z +</button>
      </div>

      <div class="stepBox" role="button" tabindex="0" on:click={() => openKeypad("Z")}>
        <span class="stepVal">{toFixedNice(stepZ)}</span>
        <span class="unit">mm</span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="hintline" style="margin-top: 12px;">
      Tik op het mm-vakje om de stapgrootte te wijzigen (touch numpad).
      Waardes worden automatisch begrensd op basis van je bereik en huidige positie.
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

{#if keypadOpen}
  <div class="overlay">
    <div class="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
      <div class="modalTitle">Stapgrootte {keypadAxis} (mm)</div>

      <input
        id="keypad-input"
        inputmode="decimal"
        bind:value={keypadValue}
        placeholder="bijv. 10"
      />

      <div class="modalHint">{keypadHint}</div>

      {#if keypadError}
        <div class="modalErr">{keypadError}</div>
      {/if}

      <div class="keypad">
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("1")}>1</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("2")}>2</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("3")}>3</button>

        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("4")}>4</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("5")}>5</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("6")}>6</button>

        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("7")}>7</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("8")}>8</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("9")}>9</button>

        <button class="kbtn secondary" on:click={() => appendKey(".")}>.</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("0")}>0</button>
        <button class="kbtn secondary" on:click={backspaceKey}>⌫</button>

        <button class="kbtn secondary" on:click={clearKey}>Clear</button>
        <button class="kbtn danger" on:click={closeKeypad}>Cancel</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={confirmKeypad}>Enter</button>
      </div>

      <div class="actions">
        <button class="kbtn secondary" on:click={closeKeypad}>Sluiten</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={confirmKeypad}>Bevestigen</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
{/if}

  .manualPage {

    gap: var(--space-4);
  }

  .manualHint {
    margin: 0;
  }

  .badgeRow {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: var(--space-2);
    margin-top: var(--space-3);
  }

  .dot {
    width: 8px;
    height: 8px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    background: #586276;
  }

  .dot.ok {
    background: #2dcc71;
  }

  .dot.bad {
    background: #e65d4a;
  }

  .manualControls {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-4);
  }

  .axisGrid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: auto 1fr auto;
    gap: var(--space-3);
    align-items: center;
  }

  .axisLabel {
    font-weight: 800;
    font-size: var(--font-size-lg);
  }

  .btns {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: var(--space-2);
  }

  .stepBox {
    background: var(--bg-surface-alt);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3);
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-2);
    cursor: pointer;
    min-width: 86px;
    justify-content: space-between;
    font-weight: 700;
  }

  .unitLabel {
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-weight: 600;
  }

  .hintline {
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
  }

  .keypadOverlay {
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55);
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    z-index: 50;
    padding: var(--space-4);
  }

  .modal {
    width: min(420px, 100%);
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: var(--space-4);
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-3);
  }

  .modalTitle {
    font-size: var(--font-size-lg);
    font-weight: 800;
    margin: 0;
  }

  .modal input {
    width: 100%;
    background: var(--bg-surface-alt);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    color: var(--text-strong);
    padding: var(--space-3);
    font-size: var(--font-size-lg);
    font-weight: 700;
  }

  .modalHint {
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
  }

  .modalErr {
    border: 1px solid #7a1f1f;
    background: #1b0d10;
    color: #ffb5b5;
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3);
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
  }

  .keypadGrid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: var(--space-2);
  }

  .kbtn {
    min-height: 44px;
    font-size: var(--font-size-lg);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  }

  .actions {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: flex-end;
    gap: var(--space-2);
  }

  @media (max-width: 620px) {
    .axisGrid {
      grid-template-columns: 1fr;
      justify-items: stretch;
      gap: var(--space-2);
    }

    .btns {
      order: 3;
      justify-content: space-between;
    }

    .stepBox {
      order: 2;
      min-width: 0;
    }
  }
</style>

  function statusPill() {
    if (status === "idle") return { txt: "Idle", cls: "pill idle" };
    if (status === "ready") return { txt: "Klaar", cls: "pill ready" };
    if (status === "waiting") return { txt: "Wacht", cls: "pill waiting" };
    if (status === "busy") return { txt: "Bezig", cls: "pill busy" };
    if (status === "success") return { txt: "Succes", cls: "pill success" };
    return { txt: "Fout", cls: "pill error" };
  }

  function machinePill() {
    if (mrState === "ready") return { txt: "Ready", cls: "mpill ok" };
    if (mrState === "printing") return { txt: "Printing", cls: "mpill warn" };
    if (mrState === "error") return { txt: "Alarm", cls: "mpill err" };
    if (mrState === "disconnected") return { txt: "Offline", cls: "mpill err" };
    return { txt: mrState, cls: "mpill" };
  }
=======
  function statusPill() {
    if (status === "idle") return { txt: "Idle", cls: "statusPill" };
    if (status === "ready" || status === "success") {
      return { txt: status === "ready" ? "Klaar" : "Succes", cls: "statusPill ok" };
    }
    if (status === "waiting" || status === "busy") {
      return { txt: status === "waiting" ? "Wacht" : "Bezig", cls: "statusPill warn" };
    }
    return { txt: "Fout", cls: "statusPill err" };
  }

  function machinePill() {
    if (mrState === "ready") return { txt: "Ready", cls: "statusPill ok" };
    if (mrState === "printing") return { txt: "Printing", cls: "statusPill warn" };
    if (mrState === "error") return { txt: "Alarm", cls: "statusPill err" };
    if (mrState === "disconnected") return { txt: "Offline", cls: "statusPill err" };
    return { txt: mrState, cls: "statusPill" };
  }

  </div>

  {#if lastError}
    <div class="errorBox">{lastError}</div>
  {/if}

  {#if view === "picker"}
    <p class="submsg">Swipe horizontaal om een vorm te kiezen en tik om te configureren.</p>

    <div class="shapeCarousel">
      {#each shapes as s}
        <div
          class={"shapeCard " + (selected === s.id ? "selected" : "")}
          on:click={() => openConfig(s.id)}
        >
          <div class="cardTop">
            <div>
              <div class="cardTitle">{s.title}</div>
              <div class="cardSub">{s.subtitle}</div>
            </div>

            <div class="miniSvg" aria-hidden="true">
              {#if s.id === "circle"}
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                  <circle cx="60" cy="60" r="32" fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="4" />
                </svg>
              {:else if s.id === "rect"}
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                  <rect x="28" y="40" width="64" height="40" rx="6" fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="4" />
                </svg>
              {:else if s.id === "slot"}
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                  <rect x="28" y="46" width="64" height="28" rx="14" ry="14" fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="4" />
                </svg>
              {:else}
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                  <polygon
                    points="60,28 86,42 86,78 60,92 34,78 34,42"
                    fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="4" />
                </svg>
              {/if}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="cardSub">Tik om te openen</div>
        </div>
      {/each}
    </div>
  {:else}
    <div class="actions">
      <input type="file" accept=".gcode" on:change={handleFileChange} />
      <button class="primary" on:click={uploadFile}>Upload Shape File</button>
      <button class="ghost" on:click={backToPicker}>← Terug</button>
      <button class="primary" on:click={prepareCut}>Vorm snijden</button>

      {#if status === "waiting"}
        <button class="primary" on:click={startCutNow}>START (test)</button>
      {/if}
    </div>

    {#if preparedSummary}
      <p class="submsg">
        Voorbereid: <b>{preparedSummary}</b>
      </p>
    {/if}

    <div class="configGrid">
      <section class="panel">
        <h2 class="panelTitle">Preview</h2>
        <div class="previewWrap">
          {#if selected === "circle"}
            <svg class="bigSvg" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
              <defs>
                <marker id="arr" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="5" refY="5" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" orient="auto-start-reverse">
                  <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="#b7c6ea"/>
                </marker>
              </defs>

              <circle cx="60" cy="60" r={mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2} fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="3" />

              <line x1={60 - mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2} y1={60 + mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2 + 14}
                    x2={60 + mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2} y2={60 + mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2 + 14}
                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr)" marker-end="url(#arr)" />
              <text x="60" y={60 + mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2 + 28} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="800">
                Ø {circle.diameter.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>
            </svg>

          {:else if selected === "rect"}
            {@const box = centerBoxSize(rect.length, rect.width)}
            <svg class="bigSvg" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
              <defs>
                <marker id="arr2" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="5" refY="5" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" orient="auto-start-reverse">
                  <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="#b7c6ea"/>
                </marker>
              </defs>

              <rect x={box.x} y={box.y} width={box.w} height={box.h} rx="6"
                    fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="3" />

              <line x1={box.x} y1={box.y + box.h + 10} x2={box.x + box.w} y2={box.y + box.h + 10}
                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr2)" marker-end="url(#arr2)" />
              <text x={box.cx} y={box.y + box.h + 24} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="800">
                L {rect.length.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>

              <line x1={box.x + box.w + 10} y1={box.y} x2={box.x + box.w + 10} y2={box.y + box.h}
                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr2)" marker-end="url(#arr2)" />
              <text x={box.x + box.w + 20} y={box.cy} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="start" font-weight="800">
                B {rect.width.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>
            </svg>

          {:else if selected === "slot"}
            {@const box = centerBoxSize(slot.length, slot.width)}
            <svg class="bigSvg" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
              <defs>
                <marker id="arr3" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="5" refY="5" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" orient="auto-start-reverse">
                  <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="#b7c6ea"/>
                </marker>
              </defs>

              <rect x={box.x} y={box.y} width={box.w} height={box.h} rx={box.h/2} ry={box.h/2}
                    fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="3" />

              <line x1={box.x} y1={box.y + box.h + 10} x2={box.x + box.w} y2={box.y + box.h + 10}
                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr3)" marker-end="url(#arr3)" />
              <text x={box.cx} y={box.y + box.h + 24} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="800">
                L {slot.length.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>

              <line x1={box.x + box.w + 10} y1={box.y} x2={box.x + box.w + 10} y2={box.y + box.h}
                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr3)" marker-end="url(#arr3)" />
              <text x={box.x + box.w + 20} y={box.cy} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="start" font-weight="800">
                B {slot.width.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>

              <text x={box.x + box.w - 2} y={box.y - 6} fill="#b7c6ea" font-size="9" text-anchor="end" font-weight="800">
                R = {(slot.width / 2).toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>
            </svg>

          {:else}
            <svg class="bigSvg" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
              <defs>
                <marker id="arr4" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="5" refY="5" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" orient="auto-start-reverse">
                  <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="#b7c6ea"/>
                </marker>
              </defs>

              <polygon points={hexPointsForPreview(hex.acrossFlats)} fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="3" />

              <text x="60" y="110" fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="800">
                S {hex.acrossFlats.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>
            </svg>
          {/if}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="panel">
        <h2 class="panelTitle">Maten</h2>

        <div class="fields">
          {#if selected === "circle"}
            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Diameter Ø (mm)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Diameter Ø (mm)",
                    value: circle.diameter,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      circle = { diameter: v };
                      normalizeCircle();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {circle.diameter.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

          {:else if selected === "rect"}
            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Lengte L (mm)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Lengte L (mm)",
                    value: rect.length,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      rect = { ...rect, length: v };
                      normalizeRect();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {rect.length.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Breedte B (mm)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Breedte B (mm)",
                    value: rect.width,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      rect = { ...rect, width: v };
                      normalizeRect();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {rect.width.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

          {:else if selected === "slot"}
            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Lengte L (mm)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Lengte L (mm)",
                    value: slot.length,
                    min: slot.width,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      slot = { ...slot, length: v };
                      normalizeSlot();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {slot.length.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Breedte B (mm) (R = B/2)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Breedte B (mm)",
                    value: slot.width,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      slot = { ...slot, width: v };
                      normalizeSlot();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {slot.width.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="hintRow">
              Radius automatisch: R = {(slot.width / 2).toFixed(1)} mm
            </div>

          {:else}
            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Steekmaat S (mm) (across flats)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Steekmaat S (mm)",
                    value: hex.acrossFlats,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      hex = { acrossFlats: v };
                      normalizeHex();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {hex.acrossFlats.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>
          {/if}

          <div class="hintRow">
            Begrensd op {XY_MAX} mm i.v.m. X/Y slag. XY moet gehomed zijn: <b>{isHomedXY() ? "JA" : "NEE"}</b>
          </div>

          {#if preparedGcode}
            <div class="hintRow">
              G-code voorbereid (preview eerste regels):
            </div>
            <div class="gcodePreview">
              {preparedGcode.split("\n").slice(0, 18).join("\n")}
            </div>
          {/if}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  {/if}
</div>

{#if padOpen}
  <div class="modalBack" on:click={padCancel}>
    <div class="modal" on:click|stopPropagation>
      <div class="modalHead">
        <div class="panelTitle">{padTitle}</div>
        <button class="ghost" on:click={padCancel}>Sluiten</button>
      </div>

      <div class="modalValue">{clamp(parseFloat(padValue || "0"), padMin, padMax).toFixed(1)} mm</div>
      <div class="submsg">Toegestaan: {padMin} – {padMax} mm</div>

      <div class="pad">
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("1")}>1</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("2")}>2</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("3")}>3</button>

        <button on:click={() => padAppend("4")}>4</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("5")}>5</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("6")}>6</button>

        <button on:click={() => padAppend("7")}>7</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("8")}>8</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("9")}>9</button>

        <button on:click={() => padAppend(".")}>.</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("0")}>0</button>
        <button on:click={padBackspace}>⌫</button>

        <button class="danger" on:click={padClear}>Clear</button>
        <button class="ghost" on:click={padCancel}>Cancel</button>
        <button class="wide primary" on:click={padOk}>Enter</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
{/if}

<style>
  :global(:root) {
    color-scheme: dark;
    --bg-body: #070b12;
    --bg-surface: #0c121d;
    --bg-surface-alt: #101828;
    --border-color: #1a2332;
    --border-color-strong: #243247;
    --accent: #6aa7ff;
    --accent-strong: #3c66c2;
    --text-strong: #e5e9f0;
    --text-medium: #bac7dd;
    --text-muted: #8895af;
    --radius-sm: 8px;
    --radius-md: 12px;
    --radius-lg: 16px;
    --space-1: 4px;
    --space-2: 6px;
    --space-3: 10px;
    --space-4: 14px;
    --space-5: 18px;
    --space-6: 24px;
    --space-7: 32px;
    --font-size-xs: 12px;
    --font-size-sm: 13px;
    --font-size-md: clamp(14px, 1.8vw, 15.5px);
    --font-size-lg: clamp(15px, 2vw, 17px);
    --font-size-xl: clamp(17px, 3vw, 20px);
  }

  :global(html, body) {
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    background: var(--bg-body);
    color: var(--text-strong);
    font-family: "Inter", system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Arial, sans-serif;
    font-size: var(--font-size-md);
    line-height: 1.45;
  }

  :global(*) {
    box-sizing: border-box;
  }

  .layout {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 56px 1fr;
    height: 100dvh;
    background: var(--bg-body);
  }

  .sidebar {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    border-right: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    padding: var(--space-4) var(--space-2);
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-3);
  }

  .logoDot {
    width: 38px;
    height: 38px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    background: radial-gradient(120% 120% at 20% 20%, #1a2436 0%, #0f1420 70%);
    border: 1px solid #1f2a3c;
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03);
    margin-bottom: var(--space-3);
  }

  .nav {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-2);
    width: 100%;
    align-items: center;
  }

  a.item {
    width: 42px;
    height: 42px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    text-decoration: none;
    color: var(--text-medium);
    border: 1px solid transparent;
    user-select: none;
    -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
    background: transparent;
    transition: background 0.14s ease, color 0.14s ease, border-color 0.14s ease;
  }

  a.item:hover {
    background: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.08);
    border-color: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.18);
    color: var(--text-strong);
  }

  a.item.active {
    background: rgba(60, 102, 194, 0.2);
    border-color: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.4);
    color: var(--text-strong);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.25) inset;
  }

  a.item:focus {
    outline: 2px solid var(--accent);
    outline-offset: 2px;
  }

  .icon {
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    display: block;
  }

  .stroke {
    stroke: currentColor;
    stroke-width: 1.8;
    fill: none;
    stroke-linecap: round;
    stroke-linejoin: round;
  }

  .fillSoft {
    fill: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
    stroke: currentColor;
    stroke-width: 1.8;
    stroke-linecap: round;
    stroke-linejoin: round;
  }

  .main {
    overflow: hidden;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-rows: 1fr;
  }

  .content {
    overflow: auto;
    padding: var(--space-5);
    width: min(100%, 760px);
    margin: 0 auto;
    -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
  }

  :global(.pageShell) {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-4);
    padding-bottom: var(--space-6);
  }

  :global(.surfaceCard) {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: var(--space-4);
    box-shadow: 0 4px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.18);
  }

  :global(.surfaceCard.tight) {
    padding: var(--space-3);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  }

  :global(.sectionTitle) {
    font-size: var(--font-size-xl);
    font-weight: 800;
    margin: 0 0 var(--space-2);
  }

  :global(.sectionLead) {
    color: var(--text-medium);
    font-size: var(--font-size-sm);
    margin: 0;
  }

  :global(.sectionHint) {
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-size: var(--font-size-sm);
    margin: 0;
  }

  :global(.statusPill),
  :global(.badgePill) {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-2);
    padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3);
    border-radius: 999px;
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    background: var(--bg-surface-alt);
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--text-medium);
  }

  :global(.statusPill.ok) {
    color: #a8f9c6;
    border-color: #215c39;
    background: rgba(33, 92, 57, 0.18);
  }

  :global(.statusPill.warn) {
    color: #ffe1a4;
    border-color: #6a5320;
    background: rgba(106, 83, 32, 0.18);
  }

  :global(.statusPill.err) {
    color: #ffb5b5;
    border-color: #7a1f1f;
    background: rgba(122, 31, 31, 0.18);
  }

  :global(.errorBox) {
    margin-top: var(--space-3);
    padding: var(--space-3);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    border: 1px solid #7a1f1f;
    background: #1b0d10;
    color: #ffb5b5;
    font-family: "JetBrains Mono", ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, "Liberation Mono", monospace;
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
    white-space: pre-wrap;
    max-height: 150px;
    overflow: auto;
  }

  :global(button) {
    background: var(--bg-surface-alt);
    color: var(--text-strong);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    padding: 10px 14px;
    font-weight: 700;
    font-size: var(--font-size-sm);
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background 0.14s ease, border-color 0.14s ease, transform 0.1s ease;
    min-height: 38px;
  }

  :global(button:hover) {
    background: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.12);
    border-color: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.3);
  }

  :global(button:active) {
    transform: translateY(1px);
  }

  :global(button:disabled) {
    opacity: 0.45;
    cursor: not-allowed;
  }

  :global(button.primary) {
    background: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.2);
    border-color: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.4);
  }

  :global(button.primary:hover) {
    background: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.28);
  }

  :global(button.secondary) {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    color: var(--text-medium);
  }

  :global(button.secondary:hover) {
    color: var(--text-strong);
  }

  :global(button.ghost) {
    background: transparent;
  }

  :global(button.danger) {
    background: rgba(122, 31, 31, 0.18);
    border-color: #7a1f1f;
    color: #ffb5b5;
  }

  :global(button.danger:hover) {
    background: rgba(122, 31, 31, 0.28);
  }

  :global(input[type="file"]) {
    max-width: 100%;
    font-size: var(--font-size-sm);
  }

  @media (max-width: 720px) {
    .layout {
      grid-template-columns: 52px 1fr;
    }

    .content {
      padding: var(--space-4);
      width: min(100%, 620px);
    }

    :global(.surfaceCard) {
      padding: var(--space-3);
    }

    :global(.sectionTitle) {
      font-size: clamp(16px, 4.2vw, 18px);
    }
  }
</style>

    <div class="statusLine" class:alarm={statusText === "Alarm"}>{statusText}</div>
    {#if statusSub}
      <div class="statusSub">{statusSub}</div>

    {#if lastError}
      <div class="errorBox">{lastError}</div>
    {/if}

      <div>Geen macro’s gevonden (nog).</div>

      </div>

      <div class="row" style="margin-top:12px">
        <button class="secondary" on:click={refreshMacros}>Refresh</button>
        <button class="secondary" on:click={cancelJob}>Cancel Job</button>

      </div>

        {#each macros as m}
          <button on:click={() => runMacro(m)}>{m}</button>
        {/each}
      </div>

      <div class="row" style="margin-top:12px">
        <button class="secondary" on:click={refreshMacros}>Refresh macro’s</button>
        <button class="secondary" on:click={cancelJob}>Cancel Job</button>
        <button class="danger" on:click={emergencyStop}>Emergency Stop</button>
      </div>
    {/if}
  </div>
</div>

      </div>

      {#if macros.length === 0}
        <p class="emptyMessage">Geen macro’s gevonden (nog).</p>
        <p class="hint">

        </div>
      {:else}
        <div class="macroList">
          {#each macros as m}
            <button class="macroButton" on:click={() => runMacro(m)}>{m}</button>
          {/each}
        </div>

        <div class="actionRow">
          <button class="secondary" on:click={refreshMacros}>Refresh macro’s</button>
          <button class="secondary" on:click={cancelJob}>Cancel Job</button>
          <button class="danger" on:click={emergencyStop}>Emergency Stop</button>
        </div>
      {/if}
    </section>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="wrap">
  <div class="card">
    <div class="title">Handbediening</div>

    <div class="hintline">
      Positie: X {toFixedNice(pos[0])} | Y {toFixedNice(pos[1])} | Z {toFixedNice(pos[2])}
      <br />
      Limits: X {positionMin[0]}..{positionMax[0]} • Y {positionMin[1]}..{positionMax[1]} • Z {positionMin[2]}..{positionMax[2]}
    </div>

    <div class="badges">
      <div class="badge">
        <span class="dot" class:ok={connected} class:bad={!connected}></span>
        {connected ? "Connected" : "Disconnected"}
      </div>

      <div class="badge">
        <span class="dot" class:ok={isAxisHomed("X")} class:bad={!isAxisHomed("X")}></span>
        X homed
      </div>
      <div class="badge">
        <span class="dot" class:ok={isAxisHomed("Y")} class:bad={!isAxisHomed("Y")}></span>
        Y homed
      </div>
      <div class="badge">
        <span class="dot" class:ok={isAxisHomed("Z")} class:bad={!isAxisHomed("Z")}></span>
        Z homed
      </div>
    </div>

    {#if lastError}
      <div class="errorBox">{lastError}</div>
    {/if}
  </div>

  <div class="card">
    <div class="title">Jog (mm)</div>

    <!-- X -->
    <div class="grid" style="margin-top: 10px;">
      <div class="axis">X</div>
      <div class="btns">
        <button disabled={!connected || !isAxisHomed("X")} on:click={() => jog("X", -1)}>X -</button>
        <button disabled={!connected || !isAxisHomed("X")} on:click={() => jog("X", 1)}>X +</button>
      </div>

      <div class="stepBox" role="button" tabindex="0" on:click={() => openKeypad("X")}>
        <span class="stepVal">{toFixedNice(stepX)}</span>
        <span class="unit">mm</span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Y -->
    <div class="grid" style="margin-top: 10px;">
      <div class="axis">Y</div>
      <div class="btns">
        <button disabled={!connected || !isAxisHomed("Y")} on:click={() => jog("Y", -1)}>Y -</button>
        <button disabled={!connected || !isAxisHomed("Y")} on:click={() => jog("Y", 1)}>Y +</button>
      </div>

      <div class="stepBox" role="button" tabindex="0" on:click={() => openKeypad("Y")}>
        <span class="stepVal">{toFixedNice(stepY)}</span>
        <span class="unit">mm</span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Z -->
    <div class="grid" style="margin-top: 10px;">
      <div class="axis">Z</div>
      <div class="btns">
        <button disabled={!connected || !isAxisHomed("Z")} on:click={() => jog("Z", -1)}>Z -</button>
        <button disabled={!connected || !isAxisHomed("Z")} on:click={() => jog("Z", 1)}>Z +</button>
      </div>

      <div class="stepBox" role="button" tabindex="0" on:click={() => openKeypad("Z")}>
        <span class="stepVal">{toFixedNice(stepZ)}</span>
        <span class="unit">mm</span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="hintline" style="margin-top: 12px;">
      Tik op het mm-vakje om de stapgrootte te wijzigen (touch numpad).
      Waardes worden automatisch begrensd op basis van je bereik en huidige positie.
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

{#if keypadOpen}
  <div class="overlay">
    <div class="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
      <div class="modalTitle">Stapgrootte {keypadAxis} (mm)</div>

      <input
        id="keypad-input"
        inputmode="decimal"
        bind:value={keypadValue}
        placeholder="bijv. 10"
      />

      <div class="modalHint">{keypadHint}</div>

      {#if keypadError}
        <div class="modalErr">{keypadError}</div>
      {/if}

      <div class="keypad">
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("1")}>1</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("2")}>2</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("3")}>3</button>

        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("4")}>4</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("5")}>5</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("6")}>6</button>

        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("7")}>7</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("8")}>8</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("9")}>9</button>

        <button class="kbtn secondary" on:click={() => appendKey(".")}>.</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={() => appendKey("0")}>0</button>
        <button class="kbtn secondary" on:click={backspaceKey}>⌫</button>

        <button class="kbtn secondary" on:click={clearKey}>Clear</button>
        <button class="kbtn danger" on:click={closeKeypad}>Cancel</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={confirmKeypad}>Enter</button>
      </div>

      <div class="actions">
        <button class="kbtn secondary" on:click={closeKeypad}>Sluiten</button>
        <button class="kbtn" on:click={confirmKeypad}>Bevestigen</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
{/if}

<style>
  .manualPage {
    max-width: 720px;
    gap: var(--space-4);
  }

  .manualHint {
    margin: 0;
  }

  .badgeRow {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: var(--space-2);
    margin-top: var(--space-3);
  }

  .dot {
    width: 8px;
    height: 8px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    background: #586276;
  }

  .dot.ok {
    background: #2dcc71;
  }

  .dot.bad {
    background: #e65d4a;
  }

  .manualControls {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-4);
  }

  .axisGrid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: auto 1fr auto;
    gap: var(--space-3);
    align-items: center;
  }

  .axisLabel {
    font-weight: 800;
    font-size: var(--font-size-lg);
  }

  .btns {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: var(--space-2);
  }

  .stepBox {
    background: var(--bg-surface-alt);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3);
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-2);
    cursor: pointer;
    min-width: 86px;
    justify-content: space-between;
    font-weight: 700;
  }

  .unitLabel {
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-weight: 600;
  }

  .hintline {
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
  }

  .keypadOverlay {
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55);
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    z-index: 50;
    padding: var(--space-4);
  }

  .modal {
    width: min(420px, 100%);
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: var(--space-4);
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: var(--space-3);
  }

  .modalTitle {
    font-size: var(--font-size-lg);
    font-weight: 800;
    margin: 0;
  }

  .modal input {
    width: 100%;
    background: var(--bg-surface-alt);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    color: var(--text-strong);
    padding: var(--space-3);
    font-size: var(--font-size-lg);
    font-weight: 700;
  }

  .modalHint {
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
  }

  .modalErr {
    border: 1px solid #7a1f1f;
    background: #1b0d10;
    color: #ffb5b5;
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3);
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
  }

  .keypadGrid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: var(--space-2);
  }

  .kbtn {
    min-height: 44px;
    font-size: var(--font-size-lg);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  }

  .actions {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: flex-end;
    gap: var(--space-2);
  }

  @media (max-width: 620px) {
    .axisGrid {
      grid-template-columns: 1fr;
      justify-items: stretch;
      gap: var(--space-2);
    }

    .btns {
      order: 3;
      justify-content: space-between;
    }

    .stepBox {
      order: 2;
      min-width: 0;
    }
  }
</style>

  function statusPill() {
    if (status === "idle") return { txt: "Idle", cls: "pill idle" };
    if (status === "ready") return { txt: "Klaar", cls: "pill ready" };
    if (status === "waiting") return { txt: "Wacht", cls: "pill waiting" };
    if (status === "busy") return { txt: "Bezig", cls: "pill busy" };
    if (status === "success") return { txt: "Succes", cls: "pill success" };
    return { txt: "Fout", cls: "pill error" };
  }

  function machinePill() {
    if (mrState === "ready") return { txt: "Ready", cls: "mpill ok" };
    if (mrState === "printing") return { txt: "Printing", cls: "mpill warn" };
    if (mrState === "error") return { txt: "Alarm", cls: "mpill err" };
    if (mrState === "disconnected") return { txt: "Offline", cls: "mpill err" };
    return { txt: mrState, cls: "mpill" };
  }
=======
  function statusPill() {
    if (status === "idle") return { txt: "Idle", cls: "statusPill" };
    if (status === "ready" || status === "success") {
      return { txt: status === "ready" ? "Klaar" : "Succes", cls: "statusPill ok" };
    }
    if (status === "waiting" || status === "busy") {
      return { txt: status === "waiting" ? "Wacht" : "Bezig", cls: "statusPill warn" };
    }
    return { txt: "Fout", cls: "statusPill err" };
  }

  function machinePill() {
    if (mrState === "ready") return { txt: "Ready", cls: "statusPill ok" };
    if (mrState === "printing") return { txt: "Printing", cls: "statusPill warn" };
    if (mrState === "error") return { txt: "Alarm", cls: "statusPill err" };
    if (mrState === "disconnected") return { txt: "Offline", cls: "statusPill err" };
    return { txt: mrState, cls: "statusPill" };
  }

      </div>

    <div class="errorBox">Klipper alarm:\n{mrMessage}</div>

    <div class={statusPill().cls}>{statusPill().txt}</div>
    <div class="submsg">{statusMsg}</div>

  {#if lastError}
    <div class="errorBox">{lastError}</div>
  {/if}

      Swipe horizontaal om een vorm te kiezen, tik om te configureren.
    </div>

    <div class="swipeRow">
      {#each shapes as s}
        <div
          class={"shapeCard " + (selected === s.id ? "selected" : "")}
          on:click={() => openConfig(s.id)}
        >
          <div class="cardTop">
            <div>
              <div class="cardTitle">{s.title}</div>
              <div class="cardSub">{s.subtitle}</div>
            </div>

            <div class="miniSvg" aria-hidden="true">
              {#if s.id === "circle"}
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                  <circle cx="60" cy="60" r="32" fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="6" />
                </svg>
              {:else if s.id === "rect"}
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                  <rect x="28" y="40" width="64" height="40" rx="6" fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="6" />
                </svg>
              {:else if s.id === "slot"}

                    fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="6" />
                </svg>

            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="cardSub">Tik om te openen</div>
        </div>
      {/each}
    </div>

<div class="pageShell shapePage">
  <div class="topbar">
    <div>
      <h1>Vorm snijden</h1>
      <p class="submsg">Max {XY_MAX}×{XY_MAX} mm, XY-only G-code. Voorwaarde: X en Y gehomed.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="rightInfo">
      <span class={machinePill().cls}>
        {machinePill().txt} · homed: {homedAxes || "-"}
      </span>
    </div>
  </div>

  {#if mrState === "error" && mrMessage}
    <div class="errorBox">Klipper alarm:\n{mrMessage}</div>
  {/if}

  <div class="statusRow">
    <span class={statusPill().cls}>{statusPill().txt}</span>
    <span class="submsg">{statusMsg}</span>
  </div>

  {#if lastError}
    <div class="errorBox">{lastError}</div>
  {/if}

  {#if view === "picker"}
    <p class="submsg">Swipe horizontaal om een vorm te kiezen en tik om te configureren.</p>

    <div class="shapeCarousel">
      {#each shapes as s}
        <div
          class={"shapeCard " + (selected === s.id ? "selected" : "")}
          on:click={() => openConfig(s.id)}
        >
          <div class="cardTop">
            <div>
              <div class="cardTitle">{s.title}</div>
              <div class="cardSub">{s.subtitle}</div>
            </div>

              {#if s.id === "circle"}

                  <circle cx="60" cy="60" r="32" fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="4" />
                </svg>
              {:else if s.id === "rect"}
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                  <rect x="28" y="40" width="64" height="40" rx="6" fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="4" />
                </svg>
              {:else if s.id === "slot"}
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                  <rect x="28" y="46" width="64" height="28" rx="14" ry="14" fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="4" />
                </svg>
              {:else}
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
                  <polygon
                    points="60,28 86,42 86,78 60,92 34,78 34,42"
                    fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="4" />
                </svg>
              {/if}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="cardSub">Tik om te openen</div>
        </div>
      {/each}
    </div>
  {:else}
    <div class="actions">
      <input type="file" accept=".gcode" on:change={handleFileChange} />
      <button class="primary" on:click={uploadFile}>Upload Shape File</button>
      <button class="ghost" on:click={backToPicker}>← Terug</button>
      <button class="primary" on:click={prepareCut}>Vorm snijden</button>

      {#if status === "waiting"}
        <button class="primary" on:click={startCutNow}>START (test)</button>
      {/if}
    </div>

    {#if preparedSummary}
      <p class="submsg">
        Voorbereid: <b>{preparedSummary}</b>
      </p>
    {/if}

    <div class="configGrid">
      <section class="panel">
        <h2 class="panelTitle">Preview</h2>

          {#if selected === "circle"}
            <svg class="bigSvg" viewBox="0 0 120 120">

                <marker id="arr" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="5" refY="5" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" orient="auto-start-reverse">
                  <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="#b7c6ea"/>
                </marker>
              </defs>

              <circle cx="60" cy="60" r={mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2} fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="3" />

              <line x1={60 - mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2} y1={60 + mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2 + 14}

                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr)" marker-end="url(#arr)" />
              <text x="60" y={60 + mmToPx(circle.diameter)/2 + 28} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="800">
                Ø {circle.diameter.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>

                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr2)" marker-end="url(#arr2)" />
              <text x={box.cx} y={box.y + box.h + 24} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="800">
                L {rect.length.toFixed(1)} mm

              <line x1={box.x} y1={box.y + box.h + 10} x2={box.x + box.w} y2={box.y + box.h + 10}
                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr3)" marker-end="url(#arr3)" />
              <text x={box.cx} y={box.y + box.h + 24} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="800">
                L {slot.length.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>

              <line x1={box.x + box.w + 10} y1={box.y} x2={box.x + box.w + 10} y2={box.y + box.h}
                    stroke="#b7c6ea" stroke-width="2" marker-start="url(#arr3)" marker-end="url(#arr3)" />
              <text x={box.x + box.w + 20} y={box.cy} fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="start" font-weight="800">
                B {slot.width.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>

              <text x={box.x + box.w - 2} y={box.y - 6} fill="#b7c6ea" font-size="9" text-anchor="end" font-weight="800">
                R = {(slot.width / 2).toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>
            </svg>

          {:else}
            <svg class="bigSvg" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
              <defs>
                <marker id="arr4" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="5" refY="5" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" orient="auto-start-reverse">
                  <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="#b7c6ea"/>
                </marker>
              </defs>

              <polygon points={hexPointsForPreview(hex.acrossFlats)} fill="none" stroke="#6aa7ff" stroke-width="3" />

              <text x="60" y="110" fill="#eaf0ff" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="800">
                S {hex.acrossFlats.toFixed(1)} mm
              </text>
            </svg>
          {/if}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="panel">
        <h2 class="panelTitle">Maten</h2>

        <div class="fields">
          {#if selected === "circle"}
            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Diameter Ø (mm)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Diameter Ø (mm)",
                    value: circle.diameter,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      circle = { diameter: v };
                      normalizeCircle();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {circle.diameter.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

          {:else if selected === "rect"}
            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Lengte L (mm)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Lengte L (mm)",
                    value: rect.length,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      rect = { ...rect, length: v };
                      normalizeRect();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {rect.length.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Breedte B (mm)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Breedte B (mm)",
                    value: rect.width,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      rect = { ...rect, width: v };
                      normalizeRect();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {rect.width.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

          {:else if selected === "slot"}
            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Lengte L (mm)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Lengte L (mm)",
                    value: slot.length,
                    min: slot.width,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      slot = { ...slot, length: v };
                      normalizeSlot();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {slot.length.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Breedte B (mm) (R = B/2)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Breedte B (mm)",
                    value: slot.width,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      slot = { ...slot, width: v };
                      normalizeSlot();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {slot.width.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="hintRow">
              Radius automatisch: R = {(slot.width / 2).toFixed(1)} mm
            </div>

          {:else}
            <div class="field">
              <div class="label">Steekmaat S (mm) (across flats)</div>
              <div
                class="valueBox"
                on:click={() =>
                  openNumpad({
                    title: "Steekmaat S (mm)",
                    value: hex.acrossFlats,
                    min: 0.1,
                    max: XY_MAX,
                    apply: (v) => {
                      hex = { acrossFlats: v };
                      normalizeHex();
                    }
                  })}
              >
                {hex.acrossFlats.toFixed(1)} mm
              </div>
            </div>
          {/if}

          <div class="hintRow">
            Begrensd op {XY_MAX} mm i.v.m. X/Y slag. XY moet gehomed zijn: <b>{isHomedXY() ? "JA" : "NEE"}</b>
          </div>

          {#if preparedGcode}
            <div class="hintRow">
              G-code voorbereid (preview eerste regels):
            </div>
            <div class="gcodePreview">
              {preparedGcode.split("\n").slice(0, 18).join("\n")}
            </div>
          {/if}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  {/if}
</div>

{#if padOpen}
  <div class="modalBack" on:click={padCancel}>
    <div class="modal" on:click|stopPropagation>
      <div class="modalHead">
        <div class="panelTitle">{padTitle}</div>
        <button class="ghost" on:click={padCancel}>Sluiten</button>
      </div>

      <div class="modalValue">{clamp(parseFloat(padValue || "0"), padMin, padMax).toFixed(1)} mm</div>
      <div class="submsg">Toegestaan: {padMin} – {padMax} mm</div>

      <div class="pad">
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("1")}>1</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("2")}>2</button>

        <button on:click={() => padAppend("4")}>4</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("5")}>5</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("6")}>6</button>

        <button on:click={() => padAppend("7")}>7</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("8")}>8</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("9")}>9</button>

        <button on:click={() => padAppend(".")}>.</button>
        <button on:click={() => padAppend("0")}>0</button>
        <button on:click={padBackspace}>⌫</button>

        <button class="danger" on:click={padClear}>Clear</button>
        <button class="ghost" on:click={padCancel}>Cancel</button>
        <button class="wide primary" on:click={padOk}>Enter</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
{/if}

<style>
  .shapePage {
    gap: var(--space-4);
  }

  .topbar {
    display: flex;
    align-items: flex-start;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: var(--space-3);
  }

  .submsg {
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-size: var(--font-size-sm);
    margin: 0;
    white-space: pre-wrap;
  }

  .rightInfo {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-2);
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    justify-content: flex-end;
  }

  .statusRow {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-2);
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }

  .statusRow .submsg {
    color: var(--text-medium);
  }

  .shapeCarousel {
    --card-width: clamp(180px, 65vw, 220px);
    display: grid;
    grid-auto-flow: column;
    grid-auto-columns: var(--card-width);
    gap: var(--space-3);
    overflow-x: auto;
    padding-bottom: var(--space-2);
    scroll-snap-type: x mandatory;
    -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
  }

  .shapeCarousel::before,
  .shapeCarousel::after {
    content: "";
    width: calc((100% - var(--card-width)) / 2);
  }

  .shapeCarousel::-webkit-scrollbar {
    height: 8px;
  }

  .shapeCarousel::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
    background: rgba(27, 40, 70, 0.8);
    border-radius: 999px;
  }

  .shapeCard {
    scroll-snap-align: center;
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: var(--space-3);
    cursor: pointer;
    user-select: none;
    transition: transform 0.14s ease, border-color 0.14s ease, box-shadow 0.14s ease;
  }

  .shapeCard:active {
    transform: scale(0.98);
  }

  .shapeCard.selected {
    border-color: rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.45);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(106, 167, 255, 0.22) inset;
  }

  .cardTop {
    display: flex;
    align-items: flex-start;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: var(--space-2);
    margin-bottom: var(--space-2);
  }

  .cardTitle {
    font-weight: 800;
    font-size: var(--font-size-lg);
  }

  .cardSub {
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
    margin-top: 2px;
  }

  .miniSvg {
    width: 70px;
    height: 70px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    background: #0b101b;
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    flex: 0 0 auto;
  }

  .actions {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: var(--space-2);
    margin-bottom: var(--space-3);
  }

  .configGrid {
    display: grid;
    gap: var(--space-3);
  }

  @media (min-width: 780px) {
    .configGrid {
      grid-template-columns: 1.1fr 0.9fr;
    }
  }

  .panel {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: var(--space-3);
  }

  .panelTitle {
    font-weight: 800;
    font-size: var(--font-size-lg);
    margin: 0 0 var(--space-2);
  }

  .previewWrap {
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    padding: var(--space-2) 0;
  }

  .bigSvg {
    width: min(100%, 360px);
    height: auto;
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    background: #0b101b;
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
  }

  .fields {
    display: grid;
    gap: var(--space-2);
  }

  .field {
    display: grid;
    gap: var(--space-1);
  }

  .label {
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
    font-weight: 700;
  }

  .valueBox {
    background: var(--bg-surface-alt);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3);
    font-weight: 750;
    cursor: pointer;
  }

  .hintRow {
    margin-top: var(--space-2);
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
  }

  .gcodePreview {
    margin-top: var(--space-2);
    padding: var(--space-2);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    border: 1px solid #223254;
    background: #0b101b;
    color: #cfe1ff;
    font-family: "JetBrains Mono", ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, "Liberation Mono", monospace;
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
    max-height: 160px;
    overflow: auto;
    white-space: pre;
  }

  .modalBack {
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55);
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    z-index: 999;
    padding: var(--space-4);
  }

  .modal {
    width: min(420px, 100%);
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: var(--space-4);
  }

  .modalHead {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: var(--space-2);
    margin-bottom: var(--space-2);
  }

  .modalValue {
    font-size: clamp(20px, 6vw, 26px);
    font-weight: 800;
    background: var(--bg-surface-alt);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3);
    text-align: right;
    margin-bottom: var(--space-2);
  }

  .pad {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: var(--space-2);
  }

  .pad button {
    min-height: 46px;
    font-size: var(--font-size-lg);
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  }

  .pad .wide {
    grid-column: span 2;
  }

  @media (max-width: 620px) {
    .topbar {
      flex-direction: column;
      align-items: flex-start;
    }

    .rightInfo {
      justify-content: flex-start;
    }
  }
